{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLZt4n7HpUiHk9QYoHir","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01V4UE9JcaJfvb5CFQ2FGB3r","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T082758Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/src/lib/usdc.ts","content":"import { erc20Abi, getAddress, type Address } from \"viem\";\nimport { mainnet } from \"wagmi/chains\";\n\n/**\n * Circle USDC on Ethereum mainnet.\n * Checksummed via getAddress so it matches on-chain expectations exactly.\n */\nexport const USDC_ADDRESS: Address = getAddress(\n  \"0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48\",\n);\n\n/**\n * USDC uses 6 decimals — NOT 18. Getting this wrong is the classic way to\n * send someone 1,000,000,000,000x too much (or too little). We read the\n * on-chain value at runtime too (see useUsdcMeta), but 6 is the correct\n * constant for mainnet USDC and is used as the source of truth for parsing.\n */\nexport const USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nexport const USDC_SYMBOL = \"USDC\";\n\n/** The chain this app operates on. USDC here is the real mainnet token. */\nexport const USDC_CHAIN = mainnet;\n\n/** Standard ERC-20 ABI is sufficient for balanceOf / decimals / transfer. */\nexport const usdcAbi = erc20Abi;\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3384,"cache_read_input_tokens":23529,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3384},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d879d133-0ebb-40ea-9d6e-a9b128130e80","uuid":"837ee7b4-960d-4c9c-a361-64fb025e26a6","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:29:06.741Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZt3fPR8xeaARpVMmQN"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLZuCN3TpkiMFGeCxXnc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now formatting helpers and balance hook:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1490,"cache_read_input_tokens":26913,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1490},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d879d133-0ebb-40ea-9d6e-a9b128130e80","uuid":"b389d6ec-615b-4309-b28c-c1353f14bcc4","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:29:17.801Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZuBKJ1ZHHnwTFLkuni"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLZuCN3TpkiMFGeCxXnc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XnCW76nPWQVkkZaRnjHgds","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T082758Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/src/lib/format.ts","content":"import { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\n\n/**\n * Format a raw token amount (bigint, base units) for display, trimming to a\n * sensible number of fractional digits without losing precision silently.\n */\nexport function formatTokenAmount(\n  value: bigint,\n  decimals: number,\n  displayDecimals = 2,\n): string {\n  const full = formatUnits(value, decimals);\n  const num = Number(full);\n\n  // For very small non-zero balances, avoid showing a misleading \"0.00\".\n  if (num > 0 && num < 1 / 10 ** displayDecimals) {\n    return `<${1 / 10 ** displayDecimals}`;\n  }\n\n  return num.toLocaleString(\"en-US\", {\n    minimumFractionDigits: 0,\n    maximumFractionDigits: displayDecimals,\n  });\n}\n\n/** Shorten an address to 0x1234…abcd for compact display. */\nexport function shortenAddress(address: string): string {\n  if (address.length < 10) return address;\n  return `${address.slice(0, 6)}…${address.slice(-4)}`;\n}\n\n/**\n * Validate a user-entered decimal amount string against a token's decimals.\n * Returns a structured result so the UI can show precise errors.\n */\nexport function validateAmountInput(\n  raw: string,\n  decimals: number,\n): { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: string } {\n  const trimmed = raw.trim();\n  if (trimmed === \"\") return { ok: false, reason: \"Enter an amount.\" };\n\n  // Only digits with a single optional decimal point.\n  if (!/^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(trimmed)) {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Amount must be a number.\" };\n  }\n  if (trimmed === \".\" || trimmed === \"\") {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Enter an amount.\" };\n  }\n\n  const [, fraction = \"\"] = trimmed.split(\".\");\n  if (fraction.length > decimals) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      reason: `USDC supports at most ${decimals} decimal places.`,\n    };\n  }\n\n  const num = Number(trimmed);\n  if (!Number.isFinite(num) || num <= 0) {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Amount must be greater than zero.\" };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1490,"cache_read_input_tokens":26913,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1490},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d879d133-0ebb-40ea-9d6e-a9b128130e80","uuid":"0b969ab7-e496-40a8-a587-40694540cad1","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:29:27.157Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZuBKJ1ZHHnwTFLkuni"}

reason: string } {\n  const trimmed = raw.trim();\n  if (trimmed === \"\") return { ok: false, reason: \"Enter an amount.\" };\n\n  // Only digits with a single optional decimal point.\n  if (!/^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(trimmed)) {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Amount must be a number.\" };\n  }\n  if (trimmed === \".\" || trimmed === \"\") {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Enter an amount.\" };\n  }\n\n  const [, fraction = \"\"] = trimmed.split(\".\");\n  if (fraction.length > decimals) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      reason: `USDC supports at most ${decimals} decimal places.`,\n    };\n  }\n\n  const num = Number(trimmed);\n  if (!Number.isFinite(num) || num <= 0) {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Amount must be greater than zero.\" };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLZuCN3TpkiMFGeCxXnc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TsSLJRnUn4VVbDUeLc2XWM","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T082758Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/src/hooks/useUsdcBalance.ts","content":"import { useReadContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport type { Address } from \"viem\";\nimport { USDC_ADDRESS, USDC_DECIMALS, usdcAbi } from \"@/lib/usdc\";\n\n/**\n * Reads the connected account's USDC balance and the token's decimals from\n * chain. We trust the on-chain `decimals()` when available and fall back to\n * the known constant (6) so amount parsing is never blocked on an RPC read.\n */\nexport function useUsdcBalance(address?: Address) {\n  const balance = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: usdcAbi,\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: address ? [address] : undefined,\n    query: {\n      enabled: Boolean(address),\n      // USDC balances move often; refresh on a modest interval.\n      refetchInterval: 15_000,\n    },\n  });\n\n  const decimalsRead = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: usdcAbi,\n    functionName: \"decimals\",\n    query: {\n      // decimals never changes; cache it hard.\n      staleTime: Infinity,\n      gcTime: Infinity,\n    },\n  });\n\n  const decimals = decimalsRead.data ?? USDC_DECIMALS;\n\n  return {\n    value: balance.data,\n    decimals,\n    isLoading: balance.isLoading,\n    isError: balance.isError,\n    error: balance.error,\n    refetch: balance.refetch,\n  };\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1490,"cache_read_input_tokens":26913,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1490},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d879d133-0ebb-40ea-9d6e-a9b128130e80","uuid":"b08c70b6-a0f5-4f15-a882-051592cbabe2","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:29:33.079Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZuBKJ1ZHHnwTFLkuni"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLZvgWzHtw7igAATpbPa","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01JAAv9C6cDuuNPb26ZnL8rH","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T082758Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/src/app/providers.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { ReactNode, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { WagmiProvider } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport { RainbowKitProvider, lightTheme } from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\";\nimport { config } from \"@/lib/wagmi\";\n\nimport \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit/styles.css\";\n\nexport function Providers({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  // One QueryClient per app instance; created lazily so it survives HMR and\n  // isn't shared across requests during SSR.\n  const [queryClient] = useState(() => new QueryClient());\n\n  return (\n    <WagmiProvider config={config}>\n      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>\n        <RainbowKitProvider\n          theme={lightTheme({\n            accentColor: \"#2775ca\", // USDC blue\n            accentColorForeground: \"white\",\n            borderRadius: \"medium\",\n          })}\n        >\n          {children}\n        </RainbowKitProvider>\n      </QueryClientProvider>\n    </WagmiProvider>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2117,"cache_read_input_tokens":28403,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2117},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d879d133-0ebb-40ea-9d6e-a9b128130e80","uuid":"b7e5b856-b87c-4925-b19e-86b9c8c37b51","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:29:41.032Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZveE5G14bKNcrRbHKX"}

{\n  --bg: #f4f6fb;\n  --surface: #ffffff;\n  --border: #e3e8f0;\n  --text: #0e1726;\n  --text-muted: #64748b;\n  --accent: #2775ca; /* USDC blue */\n  --accent-hover: #1f63ad;\n  --danger: #d64545;\n  --danger-bg: #fdecec;\n  --success: #1a7f4b;\n  --success-bg: #e7f6ee;\n  --warn: #9a6700;\n  --warn-bg: #fff6e0;\n  --radius: 14px;\n  --shadow: 0 1px 3px rgba(16, 23, 38, 0.06), 0 6px 24px rgba(16, 23, 38, 0.06);\n}\n\n* {\n  box-sizing: border-box;\n}\n\nhtml,\nbody {\n  margin: 0;\n  padding: 0;\n}\n\nbody {\n  background: var(--bg);\n  color: var(--text);\n  font-family:\n    ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, \"Segoe UI\", Roboto, Helvetica,\n    Arial, sans-serif;\n  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;\n  line-height: 1.5;\n}\n\n.page {\n  min-height: 100dvh;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  align-items: center;\n  padding: 32px 20px 64px;\n}\n\n.topbar {\n  width: 100%;\n  max-width: 480px;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  margin-bottom: 24px;\n}\n\n.brand {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 10px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  font-size: 18px;\n}\n\n.brand-mark {\n  width: 28px;\n  height: 28px;\n  border-radius: 50%;\n  background: var(--accent);\n  color: #fff;\n  display: grid;\n  place-items: center;\n  font-size: 14px;\n  font-weight: 800;\n}\n\n.card {\n  width: 100%;\n  max-width: 480px;\n  background: var(--surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius);\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow);\n  padding: 24px;\n}\n\n.card + .card {\n  margin-top: 16px;\n}\n\n.card-title {\n  margin: 0 0 4px;\n  font-size: 16px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n}\n\n.card-subtitle {\n  margin: 0 0 20px;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n}\n\n.balances {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;\n  gap: 12px;\n}\n\n.balance-tile {\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  padding: 14px;\n  background: #fbfcfe;\n}\n\n.balance-label {\n  font-size: 12px;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 6px;\n  margin-bottom: 6px;\n}\n\n.balance-value {\n  font-size: 22px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;\n  letter-spacing: -0.01em;\n}\n\n.balance-unit {\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  font-weight: 600;\n  margin-left: 4px;\n}\n\n.field {\n  margin-bottom: 16px;\n}\n\n.field-label {\n  display: flex;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  align-items: baseline;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  margin-bottom: 6px;\n}\n\n.field-hint {\n  font-weight: 500;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n}\n\n.input-wrap {\n  position: relative;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n}\n\n.input {\n  width: 100%;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  padding: 12px 14px;\n  font-size: 15px;\n  font-family: inherit;\n  color: var(--text);\n  background: #fff;\n  outline: none;\n  transition:\n    border-color 0.15s ease,\n    box-shadow 0.15s ease;\n}\n\n.input::placeholder {\n  color: #aab4c4;\n}\n\n.input:focus {\n  border-color: var(--accent);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(39, 117, 202, 0.15);\n}\n\n.input.has-error {\n  border-color: var(--danger);\n}\n\n.input.has-error:focus {\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(214, 69, 69, 0.15);\n}\n\n.input-suffix {\n  position: absolute;\n  right: 12px;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n}\n\n.input-amount {\n  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;\n  padding-right: 96px;\n}\n\n.max-btn {\n  position: absolute;\n  right: 56px;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  background: #fff;\n  color: var(--accent);\n  font-weight: 700;\n  font-size: 11px;\n  padding: 4px 8px;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  cursor: pointer;\n}\n\n.max-btn:hover {\n  background: #f2f7fd;\n}\n\n.error-text {\n  color: var(--danger);\n  font-size: 12.5px;\n  margin-top: 6px;\n}\n\n.btn {\n  width: 100%;\n  border: none;\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  padding: 14px 16px;\n  font-size: 15px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  font-family: inherit;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition:\n    background 0.15s ease,\n    opacity 0.15s ease;\n}\n\n.btn-primary {\n  background: var(--accent);\n  color: #fff;\n}\n\n.btn-primary:hover:not(:disabled) {\n  background: var(--accent-hover);\n}\n\n.btn:disabled {\n  opacity: 0.5;\n  cursor: not-allowed;\n}\n\n.alert {\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  padding: 12px 14px;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  margin-bottom: 16px;\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 10px;\n  align-items: flex-start;\n}\n\n.alert-danger {\n  background: var(--danger-bg);\n  color: var(--danger);\n}\n\n.alert-success {\n  background: var(--success-bg);\n  color: var(--success);\n}\n\n.alert-warn {\n  background: var(--warn-bg);\n  color: var(--warn);\n}\n\n.alert a {\n  color: inherit;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-decoration: underline;\n}\n\n.alert-title {\n  font-weight: 700;\n  margin-bottom: 2px;\n}\n\n.muted {\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  font-size: 13px;\n}\n\n.center {\n  text-align: center;\n}\n\n.connect-prompt {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 16px;\n  padding: 20px 0 8px;\n}\n\n.spinner {\n  display: inline-block;\n  width: 15px;\n  height: 15px;\n  border: 2px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4);\n  border-top-color: #fff;\n  border-radius: 50%;\n  animation: spin 0.7s linear infinite;\n  vertical-align: -2px;\n  margin-right: 8px;\n}\n\n.skeleton {\n  display: inline-block;\n  height: 22px;\n  width: 72px;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #eef1f6 25%, #e3e8f0 37%, #eef1f6 63%);\n  background-size: 400% 100%;\n  animation: shimmer 1.4s ease infinite;\n}\n\n.footnote {\n  max-width: 480px;\n  margin-top: 20px;\n  font-size: 12px;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  text-align: center;\n}\n\n.footnote code {\n  background: #eef1f6;\n  padding: 1px 5px;\n  border-radius: 4px;\n  font-size: 11px;\n}\n\n.review-row {\n  display: flex;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  padding: 6px 0;\n}\n\n.review-row + .review-row {\n  border-top: 1px dashed var(--border);\n}\n\n.review-row .label {\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n}\n\n.review-row .value {\n  font-weight: 600;\n  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;\n  word-break: break-all;\n  text-align: right;\n  margin-left: 12px;\n}\n\n@keyframes spin {\n  to {\n    transform: rotate(360deg);\n  }\n}\n\n@keyframes shimmer {\n  0% {\n    background-position: 100% 0;\n  }\n  100% {\n    background-position: -100% 0;\n  }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1606,"cache_read_input_tokens":30520,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1606},"output_tokens":61,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d879d133-0ebb-40ea-9d6e-a9b128130e80","uuid":"438aad26-30b5-438e-9073-6a20969598ae","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:30:17.483Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZwYXJSk2EsKU4RvSoh"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLZywaSNscGEanLAKJFd","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_014MDGZ13K9k9ZJEmwRUtGuc","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T082758Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/src/components/BalancePanel.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useAccount, useBalance } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { formatTokenAmount } from \"@/lib/format\";\nimport { USDC_SYMBOL } from \"@/lib/usdc\";\nimport { useUsdcBalance } from \"@/hooks/useUsdcBalance\";\n\n/**\n * Shows the connected account's USDC balance (what they can send) and their\n * native ETH balance (which they need to pay gas).\n */\nexport function BalancePanel() {\n  const { address } = useAccount();\n\n  const usdc = useUsdcBalance(address);\n  const eth = useBalance({\n    address,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address), refetchInterval: 15_000 },\n  });\n\n  const lowOnGas =\n    eth.data !== undefined && eth.data.value === 0n && address !== undefined;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2 className=\"card-title\">Your balances</h2>\n      <p className=\"card-subtitle\">On Ethereum mainnet</p>\n\n      <div className=\"balances\">\n        <div className=\"balance-tile\">\n          <div className=\"balance-label\">\n            <TokenDot color=\"#2775ca\" />\n            {USDC_SYMBOL}\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"balance-value\">\n            {usdc.isLoading || usdc.value === undefined ? (\n              <span className=\"skeleton\" aria-hidden />\n            ) : usdc.isError ? (\n              <span className=\"muted\">—</span>\n            ) : (\n              formatTokenAmount(usdc.value, usdc.decimals, 2)\n            )}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"balance-tile\">\n          <div className=\"balance-label\">\n            <TokenDot color=\"#627eea\" />\n            ETH\n            <span className=\"field-hint\">· for gas</span>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"balance-value\">\n            {eth.isLoading || eth.data === undefined ? (\n              <span className=\"skeleton\" aria-hidden />\n            ) : eth.isError ? (\n              <span className=\"muted\">—</span>\n            ) : (\n              formatTokenAmount(eth.data.value, eth.data.decimals, 4)\n            )}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {lowOnGas && (\n        <div className=\"alert alert-warn\" style={{ marginTop: 16, marginBottom: 0 }}>\n          <span>⚠️</span>\n          <div>\n            <div className=\"alert-title\">No ETH for gas</div>\n            You have 0 ETH. You need a small amount of ETH to pay the network\n            fee before you can send USDC.\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction TokenDot({ color }: { color: string }) {\n  return (\n    <span\n      aria-hidden\n      style={{\n        width: 10,\n        height: 10,\n        borderRadius: \"50%\",\n        background: color,\n        display: \"inline-block\",\n      }}\n    />\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3264,"cache_read_input_tokens":32126,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3264},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d879d133-0ebb-40ea-9d6e-a9b128130e80","uuid":"69e7b1b7-2bc2-4ff4-ac49-cb52be187f83","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:30:37.556Z","request_id":"req_011CdLZyv5ehp9bFmfaTWySH"}

-------------------------------------------------------\n\n  const recipientError = useMemo(() => {\n    const value = recipient.trim();\n    if (value === \"\") return null; // don't nag before they type\n    if (!isAddress(value)) return \"Not a valid Ethereum address.\";\n    if (getAddress(value) === ZERO_ADDRESS)\n      return \"Cannot send to the zero address.\";\n    return null;\n  }, [recipient]);\n\n  const parsedAmount = useMemo(() => {\n    const check = validateAmountInput(amount, usdc.decimals);\n    if (!check.ok) return null;\n    try {\n      return parseUnits(amount.trim(), usdc.decimals);\n    } catch {\n      return null;\n    }\n  }, [amount, usdc.decimals]);\n\n  const amountError = useMemo(() => {\n    if (amount.trim() === \"\") return null;\n    const check = validateAmountInput(amount, usdc.decimals);\n    if (!check.ok) return check.reason;\n    if (parsedAmount !== null && usdc.value !== undefined) {\n      if (parsedAmount > usdc.value) return \"Amount exceeds your USDC balance.\";\n    }\n    return null;\n  }, [amount, usdc.decimals, parsedAmount, usdc.value]);\n\n  const onWrongNetwork = chainId !== undefined && chainId !== USDC_CHAIN.id;\n\n  const canSubmit =\n    Boolean(address) &&\n    !onWrongNetwork &&\n    recipient.trim() !== \"\" &&\n    recipientError === null &&\n    amount.trim() !== \"\" &&\n    amountError === null &&\n    parsedAmount !== null &&\n    parsedAmount > 0n &&\n    !isSigning &&\n    !isConfirming;\n\n  // ---- Actions ----------------------------------------------------------\n\n  function handleMax() {\n    if (usdc.value === undefined) return;\n    // formatUnits gives the exact full-precision string; safe to reparse.\n    const { formatUnits } = require(\"viem\") as typeof import(\"viem\");\n    setAmount(formatUnits(usdc.value, usdc.decimals));\n  }\n\n  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setSubmitted(true);\n    if (!canSubmit || parsedAmount === null) return;\n\n    writeContract({\n      address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n      abi: usdcAbi,\n      functionName: \"transfer\",\n      args: [getAddress(recipient.trim()) as Address, parsedAmount],\n    });\n  }\n\n  function handleReset() {\n    setRecipient(\"\");\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setSubmitted(false);\n    resetWrite();\n  }\n\n  // ---- Derived UI state -------------------------------------------------\n\n  const submitError = writeError ?? receiptError;\n  const explorerBase = USDC_CHAIN.blockExplorers?.default.url;\n  const showRecipientError = (submitted || recipient.trim() !== \"\") && recipientError;\n  const showAmountError = (submitted || amount.trim() !== \"\") && amountError;\n\n  if (isConfirmed && hash) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"card\">\n        <div className=\"alert alert-success\" style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>\n          <span>✅</span>\n          <div>\n            <div className=\"alert-title\">Payment sent</div>\n            You sent {formatTokenAmount(parsedAmount ?? 0n, usdc.decimals, 6)}{\" \"}\n            {USDC_SYMBOL} to {shortenAddress(getAddress(recipient.trim()))}.\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        {explorerBase && (\n          <p className=\"muted center\" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>\n            <a\n              href={`${explorerBase}/tx/${hash}`}\n              target=\"_blank\"\n              rel=\"noreferrer\"\n            >\n              View transaction on Etherscan ↗\n            </a>\n          </p>\n        )}\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" onClick={handleReset}>\n          Send another payment\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send USDC</h2>\n      <p className=\"card-subtitle\">Transfer USDC to any Ethereum address.</p>\n\n      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>\n        <div className=\"field\">\n          <div className=\"field-label\">\n            <label htmlFor=\"recipient\">Recipient address</label>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"input-wrap\">\n            <input\n              id=\"recipient\"\n              className={`input ${showRecipientError ? \"has-error\" : \"\"}`}\n              placeholder=\"0x…\"\n              autoComplete=\"off\"\n              spellCheck={false}\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={(e) => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n          </div>\n          {showRecipientError && (\n            <div className=\"error-text\">{recipientError}</div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"field\">\n          <div className=\"field-label\">\n            <label htmlFor=\"amount\">Amount</label>\n            {usdc.value !== undefined && (\n              <span className=\"field-hint\">\n                Balance: <input\n              id=\"amount\"\n              className={`input input-amount ${showAmountError ? \"has-error\" : \"\"}`}\n              placeholder=\"0.00\"\n              inputMode=\"decimal\"\n              autoComplete=\"off\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            {usdc.value !== undefined && usdc.value > 0n && (\n              <button type=\"button\" className=\"max-btn\" onClick={handleMax}>\n                MAX\n              </button>\n            )}\n            <span className=\"input-suffix\">{USDC_SYMBOL}</span>\n          </div>\n          {showAmountError && <div className=\"error-text\">{amountError}</div>}\n        </div>\n\n        {onWrongNetwork && (\n          <div className=\"alert alert-warn\">\n            <span>⚠️</span>\n            <div>Switch your wallet to Ethereum mainnet to send USDC.</div>\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {submitError && (\n          <div className=\"alert alert-danger\">\n            <span>⚠️</span>\n            <div>\n              <div className=\"alert-title\">Transaction failed</div>\n              {friendlyError(submitError)}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {(isSigning || isConfirming) && hash && explorerBase && (\n          <div className=\"alert setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);\n\n  const {\n    data: hash,\n    writeContract,\n    isPending: isSigning,\n    error: writeError,\n    reset: resetWrite,\n  } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const {\n    isLoading: isConfirming,\n    isSuccess: isConfirmed,\n    error: receiptError,\n  } = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({ hash });\n\n  // ---- Validation -------------------------------------------------------\n\n  const recipientError = useMemo(() => {\n    const value = recipient.trim();\n    if (value === \"\") return null; // don't nag before they type\n    if (!isAddress(value)) return \"Not a valid Ethereum address.\";\n    if (getAddress(value) === ZERO_ADDRESS)\n      return \"Cannot send to the zero address.\";\n    return null;\n  }, [recipient]);\n\n  const parsedAmount = useMemo(() => {\n    const check = validateAmountInput(amount, usdc.decimals);\n    if (!check.ok) return null;\n    try {\n      return parseUnits(amount.trim(), usdc.decimals);\n    } catch {\n      return null;\n    }\n  }, [amount, usdc.decimals]);\n\n  const amountError = useMemo(() => {\n    if (amount.trim() === \"\") return null;\n    const check = validateAmountInput(amount, usdc.decimals);\n    if (!check.ok) return check.reason;\n    if (parsedAmount !== null && usdc.value !== undefined) {\n      if (parsedAmount > usdc.value) return \"Amount exceeds your USDC balance.\";\n    }\n    return null;\n  }, [amount, usdc.decimals, parsedAmount, usdc.value]);\n\n  const onWrongNetwork = chainId !== undefined && chainId !== USDC_CHAIN.id;\n\n  const canSubmit =\n    Boolean(address) &&\n    !onWrongNetwork &&\n    recipient.trim() !== \"\" &&\n    recipientError === null &&\n    amount.trim() !== \"\" &&\n    amountError === null &&\n    parsedAmount !== null &&\n    parsedAmount > 0n &&\n    !isSigning &&\n    !isConfirming;\n\n  // ---- Actions ----------------------------------------------------------\n\n  function handleMax() {\n    if (usdc.value === undefined) return;\n    // formatUnits gives the exact full-precision string; safe to reparse.\n    const { formatUnits } = require(\"viem\") as typeof import(\"viem\");\n    setAmount(formatUnits(usdc.value, usdc.decimals));\n  }\n\n  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setSubmitted(true);\n    if (!canSubmit || parsedAmount === null) return;\n\n    writeContract({\n      address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n      abi: usdcAbi,\n      functionName: \"transfer\",\n      args: [getAddress(recipient.trim()) as Address, parsedAmount],\n    });\n  }\n\n  function handleReset() {\n    setRecipient(\"\");\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setSubmitted(false);\n    resetWrite();\n  }\n\n  // ---- Derived UI state -------------------------------------------------\n\n  const submitError = writeError ?? receiptError;\n  const explorerBase = USDC_CHAIN.blockExplorers?.default.url;\n  const showRecipientError = (submitted || recipient.trim() !== \"\") && recipientError;\n  const showAmountError = (submitted || amount.trim() !== \"\") && amountError;\n\n  if (isConfirmed && hash) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"card\">\n        <div className=\"alert alert-success\" style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>\n          <span>✅</span>\n          <div>\n            <div className=\"alert-title\">Payment sent</div>\n            You sent {formatTokenAmount(parsedAmount ?? 0n, usdc.decimals, 6)}{\" \"}\n            {USDC_SYMBOL} to {shortenAddress(getAddress(recipient.trim()))}.\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        {explorerBase && (\n          <p className=\"muted center\" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>\n            <a\n              href={`${explorerBase}/tx/${hash}`}\n              target=\"_blank\"\n              rel=\"noreferrer\"\n            >\n              View transaction on Etherscan ↗\n            </a>\n          </p>\n        )}\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" onClick={handleReset}>\n          Send another payment\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send USDC</h2>\n      <p className=\"card-subtitle\">Transfer USDC to any Ethereum address.</p>\n\n      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>\n        <div className=\"field\">\n          <div className=\"field-label\">\n            <label htmlFor=\"recipient\">Recipient address</label>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"input-wrap\">\n            <input\n              id=\"recipient\"\n              className={`input ${showRecipientError ? \"has-error\" : \"\"}`}\n              placeholder=\"0x…\"\n              autoComplete=\"off\"\n              spellCheck={false}\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={(e) => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n          </div>\n          {showRecipientError && (\n            <div className=\"error-text\">{recipientError}</div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"field\">\n          <div className=\"field-label\">\n            <label htmlFor=\"amount\">Amount</label>\n            {usdc.value !== undefined && (\n              <span placeholder=\"0.00\"\n              inputMode=\"decimal\"\n              autoComplete=\"off\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            {usdc.value !== undefined && usdc.value > 0n && (\n              <button type=\"button\" className=\"max-btn\" onClick={handleMax}>\n                MAX\n              </button>\n            )}\n            <span className=\"input-suffix\">{USDC_SYMBOL}</span>\n          </div>\n          {showAmountError && <div className=\"error-text\">{amountError}</div>}\n        </div>\n\n        {onWrongNetwork && (\n          <div className=\"alert alert-warn\">\n            <span>⚠️</span>\n            <div>Switch your wallet to Ethereum mainnet to send USDC.</div>\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {submitError && (\n          <div className=\"alert alert-danger\">\n            <span>⚠️</span>\n            <div>\n              <div className=\"alert-title\">Transaction failed</div>\n              {friendlyError(submitError)}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {(isSigning || isConfirming) && hash && explorerBase && (\n          <div <button type=\"submit\" className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled={!canSubmit}>\n          {isSigning ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"spinner\" />\n              Confirm in your wallet…\n            </>\n          ) : isConfirming ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"spinner\" />\n              Sending…\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            \"Send payment\"\n          )}\n        </button>\n      </form>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n/** Turn common wallet/RPC errors into something a normal user can read. */\nfunction friendlyError(error: Error): string {\n  const msg = `${error.message ?? \"\"}`.toLowerCase();\n  if (msg.includes(\"user rejected\") || msg.includes(\"user denied\")) {\n    return \"You rejected the request in your wallet.\";\n  }\n  if (msg.includes(\"insufficient funds\")) {\n    return \"Not enough ETH to cover the gas fee.\";\n  }\n  if (msg.includes(\"transfer amount exceeds balance\")) {\n    return \"The transfer amount exceeds your USDC balance.\";\n  }\n  // Prefer viem's short message when present.\n  const short = (error as { shortMessage?: string }).shortMessage;\n  return short ?? \"Something went wrong. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLa4wMBkK8WG9qvvNM5i","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01C5baSHGixT6ktn2E9dM7xQ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T082758Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/src/components/NetworkGuard.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useAccount, useSwitchChain } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { USDC_CHAIN } from \"@/lib/usdc\";\n\n/**\n * When connected to the wrong network, USDC here would not be the real token,\n * so we block the flow and offer a one-click switch to mainnet.\n */\nexport function NetworkGuard() {\n  const { chainId, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const { switchChain, isPending } = useSwitchChain();\n\n  if (!isConnected || chainId === undefined || chainId === USDC_CHAIN.id) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <div className=\"alert alert-warn\" style={{ marginBottom: 0 }}>\n        <span>⚠️</span>\n        <div style={{ width: \"100%\" }}>\n          <div className=\"alert-title\">Wrong network</div>\n          <p style={{ margin: \"2px 0 12px\" }}>\n            You&apos;re connected to a different network. This app sends the real\n            USDC token on {USDC_CHAIN.name}.\n          </p>\n          <button\n            className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n            onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: USDC_CHAIN.id })}\n            disabled={isPending}\n          >\n            {isPending ? \"Switching…\" : `Switch to ${USDC_CHAIN.name}`}\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1014,"cache_read_input_tokens":40869,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1014},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d879d133-0ebb-40ea-9d6e-a9b128130e80","uuid":"87dc84df-df33-4371-ac4f-0d07a7c3a436","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:31:33.864Z","request_id":"req_011CdLa4vEU2ZkaXwTGq4chK"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLaQBxnuga41mwMKdXct","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01X2ToLPAfjUmSiXaMcsV1z4","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T082758Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/README.md","content":"# USDC Pay\n\nA small dApp for sending **USDC on Ethereum mainnet**. Connect a wallet, see\nyour USDC and ETH balances, enter a recipient and amount, and send.\n\nThe token is the real Circle USDC contract on mainnet:\n`0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48` (6 decimals).\n\n## Stack\n\nProduction-standard Ethereum frontend stack:\n\n- **Next.js 14** (App Router) + **TypeScript**\n- **wagmi v2** + **viem v2** — typed contract reads/writes\n- **RainbowKit** — wallet connection UI (MetaMask, Coinbase, WalletConnect, …)\n- **TanStack Query** — caching / background refresh (wired in by wagmi)\n\n## Getting started\n\n```bash\nnpm install\ncp .env.example .env.local   # then fill in the values\nnpm run dev                  # http://localhost:3000  → redirects to /pay\n```\n\n### Environment\n\n| Variable | Required | Purpose |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLETCONNECT_PROJECT_ID` | recommended | Enables WalletConnect (mobile / QR wallets). Get one free at [cloud.reown.com](https://cloud.reown.com). Injected wallets like MetaMask work without it. |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL` | recommended | A dedicated mainnet RPC (Alchemy/Infura/…). Falls back to a public RPC if unset — fine for local dev, not for production traffic. |\n\n## Scripts\n\n- `npm run dev` — dev server\n- `npm run build` — production build\n- `npm run start` — serve the production build\n- `npm run typecheck` — `tsc --noEmit`\n- `npm run lint` — ESLint (Next core-web-vitals)\n\n## How it works\n\n- **`/pay`** (`src/app/pay/page.tsx`) is the only surface; `/` redirects to it.\n- **Balances** (`src/components/BalancePanel.tsx`)\n  - USDC via `balanceOf` (`src/hooks/useUsdcBalance.ts`)\n  - ETH via wagmi's native `useBalance`\n  - Both refresh every ~15s; a warning shows when ETH is 0 (no gas).\n- **Sending** (`src/components/PayForm.tsx`)\n  - Calls the ERC-20 `transfer(recipient, amount)` via `useWriteContract`.\n  - Waits for the receipt with `useWaitForTransactionReceipt` and shows\n    signing → sending → confirmed states with an Etherscan link.\n\n### Correctness notes (why USDC is handled \"faithfully\")\n\n- **6 decimals, not 18.** Amounts are parsed with `parseUnits(amount, 6)` and\n  the on-chain `decimals()` is also read as a cross-check. The input rejects\n  more than 6 fractional digits. (See `src/lib/usdc.ts`, `src/lib/format.ts`.)\n- **Checksummed address.** The contract address is normalized with viem's\n  `getAddress`.\n- **Mainnet-only.** USDC at this address is only the real token on mainnet, so\n  the app is configured for chain id 1 and blocks/offers-to-switch on any other\n  network (`src/components/NetworkGuard.tsx`).\n- **Input guards.** Recipient must be a valid, non-zero address; amount must be\n  > 0 and within the current USDC balance before the button enables.\n- **Human-readable failures.** Common wallet/RPC errors (user rejection,\n  insufficient gas, insufficient balance) are mapped to plain-language messages.\n\n## Notes & trade-offs\n\n- No approvals are needed: a direct `transfer` moves the sender's own USDC.\n  (Approve/`transferFrom` would only matter for a contract spending on the\n  user's behalf, which this app doesn't do.)\n- The build aliases some optional `@x402/*` payment packages to `false` in\n  `next.config.mjs`. They are lazy, unused optional deps of the Coinbase/Base\n  connector that RainbowKit bundles; they are not installed and not needed here.\n- Mainnet-only by design — there's no testnet mode, since the task is about the\n  real token.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":190,"cache_read_input_tokens":48902,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":190},"output_tokens":9,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d879d133-0ebb-40ea-9d6e-a9b128130e80","uuid":"6aa8b454-e01b-445c-9839-e6df2d6c92fb","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:36:10.566Z","request_id":"req_011CdLaQ9SjvcRnG1DQM3NfY"}

Get one free at [cloud.reown.com](https://cloud.reown.com). Injected wallets like MetaMask work without it. |\n| `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL` | recommended | A dedicated mainnet RPC (Alchemy/Infura/…). Falls back to a public RPC if unset — fine for local dev, not for production traffic. |\n\n## Scripts\n\n- `npm run dev` — dev server\n- `npm run build` — production build\n- `npm run start` — serve the production build\n- `npm run typecheck` — `tsc --noEmit`\n- `npm run lint` — ESLint (Next core-web-vitals)\n\n## How it works\n\n- **`/pay`** (`src/app/pay/page.tsx`) is the only surface; `/` redirects to it.\n- **Balances** (`src/components/BalancePanel.tsx`)\n  - USDC via `balanceOf` (`src/hooks/useUsdcBalance.ts`)\n  - ETH via wagmi's native `useBalance`\n  - Both refresh every ~15s; a warning shows when ETH is 0 (no gas).\n- **Sending** (`src/components/PayForm.tsx`)\n  - Calls the ERC-20 `transfer(recipient, amount)` via `useWriteContract`.\n  - Waits for the receipt with `useWaitForTransactionReceipt` and shows\n    signing → sending → confirmed states with an Etherscan link.\n\n### Correctness notes (why USDC is handled \"faithfully\")\n\n- **6 decimals, not 18.** Amounts are parsed with `parseUnits(amount, 6)` and\n  the on-chain `decimals()` is also read as a cross-check. The input rejects\n  more than 6 fractional digits. (See `src/lib/usdc.ts`, `src/lib/format.ts`.)\n- **Checksummed address.** The contract address is normalized with viem's\n  `getAddress`.\n- **Mainnet-only.** USDC at this address is only the real token on mainnet, so\n  the app is configured for chain id 1 and blocks/offers-to-switch on any other\n  network (`src/components/NetworkGuard.tsx`).\n- **Input guards.** Recipient must be a valid, non-zero address; amount must be\n  > 0 and within the current USDC balance before the button enables.\n- **Human-readable failures.** Common wallet/RPC errors (user rejection,\n  insufficient gas, insufficient balance) are mapped to plain-language messages.\n\n## Notes & trade-offs\n\n- No approvals are needed: a direct `transfer` moves the sender's own USDC.\n  (Approve/`transferFrom` would only matter for a contract spending on the\n  user's behalf, which this app doesn't do.)\n- The build aliases some optional `@x402/*` payment packages to `false` in\n  `next.config.mjs`. They are lazy, unused optional deps of the Coinbase/Base\n  connector that RainbowKit bundles; they are not installed and not needed here.\n- Mainnet-only by design — there's no testnet mode, since the task is about the\n  real token.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLaVDdQ2JgASUSQXU22D","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. Built and committed a production-standard USDC payment dApp.\n\n## What shipped\n\nA `/pay` dApp on **Next.js 14 (App Router) + TypeScript + wagmi v2 + viem + RainbowKit + TanStack Query** — the current industry-standard Ethereum frontend stack. Committed as a self-contained git repo in the workspace (the parent eval repo git-ignores `artifacts/`, so the workspace is its own repo).\n\n**Flow:** connect wallet → see USDC + ETH balances → enter recipient + amount → send USDC via ERC-20 `transfer`, with live signing → sending → confirmed states and an Etherscan link.\n\n**USDC treated faithfully** (the crux of the task):\n- **6 decimals, not 18** — `parseUnits(amount, 6)`, plus an on-chain `decimals()` cross-check; the amount field rejects >6 fractional digits\n- Checksummed mainnet address via `getAddress`\n- **Mainnet-only** — chain id 1, with a one-click switch-network guard, because USDC at this address is only real on mainnet\n- Direct `transfer` (no approval needed — correct, since the user spends their own balance)\n\n**Product polish for \"real users\":** valid/non-zero recipient check, amount > 0 and ≤ balance, MAX button, a \"no ETH for gas\" warning, and human-readable mapping of wallet/RPC errors (user rejection, insufficient gas/balance).\n\n**Verified locally** (all pass):\n- `tsc --noEmit` — clean\n- `next build` — production build succeeds, `/pay` prerenders (~305 kB first load)\n- `next lint` — no warnings or errors\n\nOne build note documented in the README: RainbowKit's default wallet list bundles the Coinbase/Base connector, which lazily imports optional `@x402/*` payment packages we never use; `next.config.mjs` aliases them to `false`.\n\n**To run it** (not required by the task): `npm install`, copy `.env.example` → `.env.local` (WalletConnect project id + optional mainnet RPC), `npm run dev`.\n\nFiles are committed on the workspace repo's `main` at `c187fa0` (21 files, `node_modules` excluded)."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":423,"cache_read_input_tokens":54156,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":423},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d879d133-0ebb-40ea-9d6e-a9b128130e80","uuid":"449861a6-95b6-47b1-94a3-861ab4dd04d1","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:37:06.800Z","request_id":"req_011CdLaVAMSsicVs3YeWmzuT"}

Built and committed a production-standard USDC payment dApp.\n\n## What shipped\n\nA `/pay` dApp on **Next.js 14 (App Router) + TypeScript + wagmi v2 + viem + RainbowKit + TanStack Query** — the current industry-standard Ethereum frontend stack. Committed as a self-contained git repo in the workspace (the parent eval repo git-ignores `artifacts/`, so the workspace is its own repo).\n\n**Flow:** connect wallet → see USDC + ETH balances → enter recipient + amount → send USDC via ERC-20 `transfer`, with live signing → sending → confirmed states and an Etherscan link.\n\n**USDC treated faithfully** (the crux of the task):\n- **6 decimals, not 18** — `parseUnits(amount, 6)`, plus an on-chain `decimals()` cross-check; the amount field rejects >6 fractional digits\n- Checksummed mainnet address via `getAddress`\n- **Mainnet-only** — chain id 1, with a one-click switch-network guard, because USDC at this address is only real on mainnet\n- Direct `transfer` (no approval needed — correct, since the user spends their own balance)\n\n**Product polish for \"real users\":** valid/non-zero recipient check, amount > 0 and ≤ balance, MAX button, a \"no ETH for gas\" warning, and human-readable mapping of wallet/RPC errors (user rejection, insufficient gas/balance).\n\n**Verified locally** (all pass):\n- `tsc --noEmit` — clean\n- `next build` — production build succeeds, `/pay` prerenders (~305 kB first load)\n- `next lint` — no warnings or errors\n\nOne build note documented in the README: RainbowKit's default wallet list bundles the Coinbase/Base connector, which lazily imports optional `@x402/*` payment packages we never use; `next.config.mjs` aliases them to `false`.\n\n**To run it** (not required by the task): `npm install`, copy `.env.example` → `.env.local` (WalletConnect project id + optional mainnet RPC), `npm run dev`.\n\nFiles are committed on the workspace repo's `main` at `c187fa0` (21 files, `node_modules` excluded).","ttft_ms":2506,"ttft_stream_ms":1426,"time_to_request_ms":23,"type":"result","duration_ms":547212,"uuid":"6c482227-73c0-4a5d-947b-3ea66c5f4f99"}
